Where is file 3 ? why is it not in git repo ? ^dQogdVLJ

- This is not a mistake or a bug, but this senario shows 
the power of git in truly controlling and tracking changes ^FaLwtRCV

- In this scenario, the developer of the website was not sure whether the contacts in file 3 had been edited correctly or not. So he decided not to commit the changes in file 3 until he was confident about them. As a result, file 3 remained in the index as a possible change that could later be committed to the Git repo.
